### [1] [Celestial Holography, A Hitchhiker's Guide to the Celestial Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07568)
*Faïçal Barzi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了天球全息（Celestial Holography）的基本框架，旨在将渐近平坦时空中的量子引力描述为定义在天球上的二维共形场论（CCFT），核心工具是Mellin变换，将四维散射振幅映射为天球上的关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建渐近平坦时空（如Minkowski空间）中量子引力的全息描述，弥补AdS/CFT在非紧致边界情形下的缺失；探索BMS对称性与软定理、记忆效应之间的深层联系。

Method: 基于Bondi-Sachs坐标系与零无穷远（ℐ±）几何，利用Mellin变换将四维外质壳态（on-shell states）映射为天球S²上的共形初态（conformal primary wavefunctions），从而建立散射振幅到2D CCFT关联函数的字典。

Result: 确立了天球全息的核心映射机制：任意自旋粒子的渐近态经Mellin变换后成为具有确定共形权（Δ, J）的算符；BMS对称性被识别为CCFT的全局/局域对称性；软光子/胶子定理对应CCFT中的Ward恒等式。

Conclusion: 天球全息提供了一种将四维渐近平坦时空的S矩阵重铸为二维共形场论结构的自洽方案，是通向平坦空间量子引力全息原理的关键候选路径；其数学基础（如共形软定理、无穷小BMS代数表示）已初步建立，但CCFT的完整构造（如OPE、动力学）仍是开放问题。

Abstract: This primer offers an first introduction to Celestial Holography, a rapidly evolving framework aiming to describe quantum gravity in asymptotically flat spacetimes. We start from the established foundation of the AdS/CFT correspondence before venturing into the wilds of Minkowski space. The journey covers the essential Bondi-Sachs formalism and the geometry of null infinity ($\mathscr{I}^\pm$), the discovery of the infinite-dimensional BMS asymptotic symmetry group. We explain the core holographic map given by Mellin transform that recasts four-dimensional scattering amplitudes as correlation functions of a conjectured 2D Celestial Conformal Field Theory (CCFT) on the celestial sphere. The presentation is made intuitive but the mathematics is still precise (hopefully!). It is designed to equip newcomers with both the conceptual landscape and the technical vocabulary to explore this exciting frontier.

</details>


### [2] [Three-loop onset of the wormhole length in DSSYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07634)
*Eleonora Alfinito,Matteo Beccaria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了正弦-稀释子引力中爱因斯坦-罗森桥长度与双标度SYK（DSSYK）模型Krylov传播复杂度之间的精确对应，通过三圈计算给出了热场双重态下桥长初值L₀的闭式表达式，并揭示其微观解释为热态平均弦数乘以双标度参数λ；同时发展了处理发散抵消、高阶递推及低温Schwarzian展开的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立全息对偶中几何量（ER桥长度）与量子复杂度（Krylov spread complexity）之间的精确、可计算对应，并解决初值L₀中全息重整化方案依赖的模糊性问题。

Method: 在正弦-稀释子引力框架下计算disk-level ER桥长度；对DSSYK模型进行三圈微扰计算：一环已知，二环采用共点两点函数的鞍点分析并处理发散抵消，三环引入精确递推关系生成高温展开；低温极限下重组织为Schwarzian耦合λβ的展开；同步计算长度方差与三阶累积量至两环。

Result: 得到L₀的三圈闭式结果；证明L₀ = λ × 热态平均弦数，赋予其微观确定性；验证二环发散抵消机制；确认三环递推Ansatz；确立低温下各环贡献β的幂次，形成λβ展开；给出长度方差与三阶累积量的两环 semiclassical 展开。

Conclusion: ER桥长度在disk level完全编码DSSYK的Krylov复杂度结构，L₀具有普适微观定义，且高阶量子修正可通过系统微扰与递推技术精确获得，强化了复杂度/几何对偶的坚实性与可计算性。

Abstract: The length of the Einstein-Rosen bridge in sine-dilaton gravity at disk level equals the Krylov spread complexity of the dual double-scaled SYK (DSSYK) model, with the double-scaling parameter $λ$ controlling the semiclassical expansion. We compute the onset value, $L_{0}$, in the thermofield double state at $t=0$ and arbitrary inverse temperature $β$, through three loops and in closed form, extending the known one-loop result. The quantity $L_{0}$ represents the preparation complexity of the initial thermal Hartle-Hawking state. In DSSYK, $L_{0}$ is $λ$ times the average chord number of the thermal state, providing a microscopic, unambiguous determination of an additive constant otherwise fixed only by a choice of holographic renormalization scheme. The two-loop contribution follows from a saddle point evaluation of the DSSYK two-point function at coincident insertion points, where individually divergent contributions cancel only in their sum. At three loops, we bypass the saddle point analysis using an exact recursion relation that generates long high-temperature expansions at low cost, fixing and testing a proposed Ansatz against many independent data points. Finally, in the low-temperature regime each loop order contributes one further power of $β$, reorganizing the semiclassical series into an expansion in the Schwarzian coupling $λβ$, whose leading coefficient we check against an independent one-loop Schwarzian computation. The same methods, applied to the length variance and third-order cumulant at $t=0$, give their semiclassical expansion through two loops.

</details>


### [3] [Towards a Holographic dual of Carrollian BCFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07658)
*Pronoy Chakraborty,Ritankar Chatterjee,Priyadarshini Pandit*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Carrollian框架构建平直时空中的AdS₃/BCFT₂对应关系，识别出边界Carroll共形代数（BCCA）作为平直时空零无穷远处带端界（EOW）brane的对称代数，并验证其全局与渐近对称性完全匹配BCCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立平直时空版本的AdS₃/BCFT₂对应，弥补现有AdS背景下边界共形场论（BCFT）与全息对偶在平直时空中的理论缺口。

Method: 基于Carrollian极限与边界Carroll共形代数（BCCA）结构，结合端界（EOW）brane的内在几何分析及其从AdS情形取平直极限的方法，研究平直时空零无穷处的对称性实现。

Result: 确认BCCA可实现为平直时空零无穷处带适当EOW brane的对称代数；该brane的全局与渐近对称性严格等同于BCCA；EOW brane性质可通过Carroll极限从AdS情形导出。

Conclusion: Carrollian框架成功实现了平直时空下类AdS₃/BCFT₂的全息结构，BCCA成为连接平直边界共形场论与带端界引力的自然对称性基础。

Abstract: In this work we aim to lay the foundation of flat version of AdS$_3$/BCFT$_2$ correspondence through Carrollian framework. We begin with discussing the recently discovered Boundary Carrollian Conformal Algebra (BCCA), the symmetry algebra of Carrollian BCFT. We identify this algebra at the null infinity of flat spacetime with appropriate choice of the end-of-the-world (EOW) brane. The properties of this EOW brane have been analysed both intrinsically and by taking flat limit from its AdS counterpart. We also find that the global as well as asymptotic symmetries of flat spacetime restricted by this EOW brane coincide exactly with the symmetries of BCCA.

</details>


### [4] [Superconformal indices and black hole saddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07660)
*Maciej Kolanowski,Donald Marolf,Zi-Yue Wang,Wenwen Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AdS/CFT框架下N=4 SYM超共形指数在大N极限下的鞍点结构，指出在Im τ, Im σ → ∞极限下指数趋近于1，从而排除了指数级大N黑洞鞍点的贡献；提出一种基于实洛伦兹符号、含余维-2奇点的路径积分定义，并发现该方法自动满足上述界限，且由Stokes现象实现该约束；类似结论亦适用于ABJM理论在AdS₄中的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超共形指数在大N极限下如何由全息对偶中的引力鞍点重构，特别是澄清此前提出的黑洞型鞍点是否真正在强耦合、高温度（大虚部化学势）极限下主导指数行为。

Method: 结合全息对偶、鞍点分析、复几何路径积分定义（强调实洛伦兹度规与余维-2奇点）、Stokes现象解析延拓技术，对N=4 SU(N) SYM和ABJM理论的超共形指数进行渐近分析。

Result: 证明在Im τ, Im σ → ∞极限下，超共形指数I → 1，故任何O(e^{cN²})型黑洞鞍点贡献被压制；唯一允许的鞍点必须满足该指数衰减约束；路径积分须定义在具特定奇点结构的实洛伦兹时空上，且Stokes现象自然施加该约束。

Conclusion: 标准欧氏黑洞鞍点不参与超共形指数的大N渐近行为；正确的全息再现需采用具物理奇点的洛伦兹路径积分框架，并由Stokes现象保障一致性；该机制具有普适性，延伸至AdS₄/ABJM情形。

Abstract: The AdS/CFT correspondence implies that the superconformal index ${\mathcal I}$ in ${\mathcal N=4}$ SU(N) supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory can be computed using the dual bulk theory. In particular, in the limit of large $N$, the index should be given by a sum over appropriate saddles. However, ${\mathcal I}$ depends on potentials $σ, τ, \vec Δ$ and, at large ${\rm Im}\, τ= {\rm Im}\, σ$, the CFT index ${\mathcal I}$ rapidly approaches $1$ at all values of $N$. As a result, black hole saddles associated with exponentially large contributions in $N$ cannot contribute in this limit. This in particular excludes saddles that were previously suggested to be relevant in such regimes. We thus consider an approach to the bulk path integral motivated by taking it to be defined as an integral over real Lorentz-signature spacetimes with codimension-2 singularities. This approach leads only to saddles that satisfy the above bound, and to the enforcement of this bound via Stokes phenomena. We also find similar results for bulk AdS$_4$ calculations of the ABJM superconformal index.

</details>


### [5] [R7-branes and AdS$_{\text{9}}$ solutions in type IIB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07664)
*Giuseppe Dibitetto,Cameron Gibson,Nicolò Petri,Colin Sterckx*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了IIB型超引力中最近发现的AdS₉背景，通过引入更便利的坐标系计算全息中心电荷等可观测量；构造了以这些AdS₉为近视界几何的非超对称7-膜解析解；并指出其在横截面施加反射单值性τ→−τ̄时与R7膜相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS₉背景的全息对偶及其实现机制，特别是通过非超对称7-膜构型实现该背景，并探索其与已知R7膜结构的联系。

Method: 采用适配的坐标系重参数化AdS₉解；解析求解IIB轴子-胀子场驱动的非超对称7-膜方程；分析横截面单值性条件τ→−τ̄对解的约束。

Result: 给出了AdS₉背景下的全息中心电荷显式表达；构造出具有AdS₉近视界极限的解析非超对称7-膜解；证明在反射单值性条件下，该解退化为R7膜构型。

Conclusion: AdS₉背景可通过非超对称7-膜实现，且其单值性结构蕴含R7膜的几何本质，为高维AdS/共形场论对偶提供了新实例。

Abstract: We consider the recently found $\mathrm{AdS}_9$ backgrounds in type IIB supergravity. We start by reviewing the solutions in a more convenient set of coordinates, which allows us to explicitly compute a few relevant observables, including the holographic central charge. Subsequently, we study non-supersymmetric 7-brane configurations sourced by the IIB axio-dilaton. We find explicit analytic 7-brane solutions that possess the above $\mathrm{AdS}_9$ backgrounds as their near-horizon geometry. Finally, we discuss the relation of these 7-brane solutions to the R7 branes by imposing the reflection monodromy $τ\rightarrow -\bar τ$ in the transverse plane.

</details>


### [6] [Updates on Calabi-Yau manifolds from pairs of non-compact Calabi-Yau manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07694)
*Nam-Hoon Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了通过光滑法构造Calabi-Yau流形的最新进展，特别是基于拟Fano流形的法向交叉并集的光滑化方法；重点包括第二Betti数无界、Hodge数极小的Calabi-Yau三维流形，以及首例高维非Kähler Calabi-Yau例子，并阐释了Landau-Ginzburg模型在镜像对称构造中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为拓展Calabi-Yau流形的构造方法，满足弦论紧化与镜像对称研究中对拓扑多样性（如Betti数、Hodge数）及几何刚性（如Kähler性）的需求。

Method: 采用法向交叉并集（normal crossing union）的光滑化技术，以拟Fano流形为构件构造Calabi-Yau流形；结合代数几何中的变形理论与镜像对称中的Landau-Ginzburg模型。

Result: 构造出第二Betti数无界的Calabi-Yau三维流形；获得Hodge数极小（如h^{1,1}或h^{2,1}趋近于零）的实例；首次实现维度大于三的非Kähler Calabi-Yau流形。

Conclusion: 该光滑化方法显著扩展了Calabi-Yau流形的构造谱系，尤其在拓扑可控性与几何非Kähler性方面取得突破，为弦论紧化与镜像对称提供了新类别的背景时空。

Abstract: We discuss developments in the construction of Calabi--Yau manifolds by smoothing normal crossing unions of quasi-Fano manifolds, following the introduction of this method to the physics community in 2010. We describe constructions of Calabi--Yau threefolds with unbounded second Betti numbers and with very small Hodge numbers, as well as the first non-Kähler examples in dimensions greater than three. The role of Landau--Ginzburg models in mirror constructions is also explained.

</details>


### [7] [Supertranslations are Soft Dressings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07699)
*Asaad Elkhidir,Donal O'Connell,Radu Roiban*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在电动力学和引力中，由于缺乏质量间隙，大质量粒子的渐近态并非唯一定义的问题；作者利用KMOC形式论，探讨了经典层面下粒子态‘dress’（即相干态修饰）的自由度，并揭示其与大规范/BMS变换之间的联系；通过构造守恒荷（如引力中的BMS荷），表明可观测量仅依赖于大规范/BMS参数的高阶多极部分，而与单极、偶极部分无关；该结构源于软因子化而非局域对称性，并在无规范对称性的标量理论中亦被验证；最后，作者分析了大规范变换对两类粒子散射态的作用，得到普适的撞击参数位移，并厘清辐射角动量的参考系依赖性与总角动量的参考系无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决电动力学与引力中因无质量间隙导致的大质量粒子渐近态非唯一性问题，理解其对经典可观测量（如散射振幅、撞击参数、角动量）的影响机制，并厘清大规范/BMS变换、软物理与守恒荷之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用KMOC（Kosower-Maybee-O'Connell）经典散射形式论，构建基于相干态的粒子态dressings，将dress参数识别为大规范/BMS变换参数；通过软因子化结构导出可观测量的变换律，构造对应守恒荷（如BMS电荷），并在标量、阿贝尔规范场及引力三种中介子模型中统一分析两体散射态下的大变换作用。

Result: 1) 相干态dress等价于大规范/BMS变换，其位移算符可延拓为守恒荷（如引力中BMS电荷）；2) 可观测量对dress参数的单极与偶极部分不敏感（全阶成立）；3) 大变换引起普适的撞击参数位移；4) 辐射角动量具有参考系依赖性，而总角动量守恒且参考系无关；5) 标量理论中复现相同结构，表明该机制源于软因子化而非局域规范对称性。

Conclusion: 大质量粒子的渐近态自由度本质上由软辐射的红外结构决定，其经典实现体现为大规范/BMS变换，可观测量的红外安全需剔除单极与偶极模式；该图像统一适用于规范理论与引力，并可脱离局域对称性框架，仅依赖软定理与因子化结构。

Abstract: Asymptotic states of massive particles in electrodynamics and gravity are not uniquely defined because of the absence of a mass gap. Working in the KMOC formalism, we study the classical implications of this freedom in the dressing of massive particle states. We show that coherent-state dressings modify observables by a large gauge/BMS transformation. The associated coherent-state displacement operator can be completed into a conserved charge, the BMS charge in the gravitational case, which implements large gauge transformations on scattering data. The parameter of our dressing is directly the large gauge/BMS transformation parameter, and we explain that observables do not depend on monopolar and dipolar parts of the transformation parameter to all orders. Our construction is driven by soft factorization rather than by an underlying local symmetry, and we illustrate this by exhibiting the same structure in a scalar theory with no gauge symmetry. We explore the action of large gauge transformations on two-particle states for scalar, abelian gauge and graviton mediators, obtaining a universal shift of the impact parameter and clarifying the frame dependence of the radiated angular momentum vis à vis the frame-independence of the total angular momentum. The transformation connecting the canonical BMS frame, in which the Schwarzschild metric takes the Kerr-Schild form, to the intrinsic frame, in which it takes the De Donder form, illustrates these effects concretely.

</details>

Abstract: We show that black hole horizons with non-constant curvature can be constructed in any even dimension, when the source of the Einstein equations is given by the stress-energy tensor of a non-linear sigma model of a particular class

### [9] [Incidence Relations for Self-Dual Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07774)
*Joon-Hwi Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了自对偶黑洞时空（Eguchi-Hanson、自对偶Taub-NUT、自对偶Plebański-Demiański）在Kerr-Schild坐标下的显式twistor入射关系，通过Dunajski-Mason递归求解Plebański第二天堂方程，得到闭式解，并指出自对偶Taub-NUT的入射关系线性依赖于引力耦合常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在Penrose非线性引力子框架下显式构造自对偶黑洞时空的twistor几何结构，特别是其模空间与入射关系的解析表达。

Method: 采用Dunajski-Mason递归方法求解Plebański第二天堂方程，结合Kerr-Schild坐标系与twistor理论，导出各典型自对偶黑洞解的holomorphic twistor线入射关系。

Result: 获得Eguchi-Hanson、自对偶Taub-NUT及自对偶Plebański-Demiański度规的闭式入射关系；其中自对偶Taub-NUT的入射关系显式线性依赖于牛顿常数G；并给出Plebański标量的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 证实自对偶黑洞时空可由twistor几何完全刻画，且其入射关系具有简洁解析结构，为零质量场在自对偶背景上的构造提供了几何基础。

Abstract: In Penrose's nonlinear graviton construction, a self-dual spacetime arises as the moduli space of holomorphic twistor lines in curved twistor space, whose explicit parametrization is concretely given by the incidence relation. We demonstrate a concrete local construction of the incidence relations for self-dual black hole spacetimes in Kerr-Schild coordinates: Eguchi-Hanson, self-dual Taub-NUT, and self-dual Plebański-Demiański. This is facilitated by implementing the Dunajski-Mason recursion in the framework of Plebański's second heavenly equation, for which we explicitly find the Plebański scalar. Our results describe closed-form formulae. Notably, for the self-dual Taub-NUT solution, the exact incidence relation exhibits linear dependence on the gravitational coupling. The construction of zero-rest-mass fields on self-dual black hole backgrounds is briefly sketched as an application.

</details>


### [10] [Four-Fermion Condensates in Curved Spacetimes: A Functional Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07907)

In strong interactions, pions and kaons are the (would-be) Nambu-Goldstone bosons and experiments underway or planned at existing or anticipated high-energy, high-luminosity facilities will gather data that it is hoped will enable maps to be drawn of their internal structure. Meanwhile, theory and phenomenology find themselves in something of a quagmire. For finite chemical potential ($μ$) and a physical strange mass ($m_s=95$ MeV) with massless light quarks, the transition remains second-order, with $T_c$ decreasing as $μ$ increases. Abstract: We started the Living Review of Machine Learning for Particle Physics (HEP-ML Living Review) in 2020 as a community-maintained, near-comprehensive bibliography of machine learning in particle physics. Since then the literature has grown by more than an order of magnitude, the methods reach far beyond the classification and generation tasks of the early years, and the community has built its own ecosystem of topic-specific reviews, benchmark papers, and software frameworks. The original model no longer serves this field well, and we can no longer sustain it. We therefore change direction. We freeze the Living Review as an archival reference covering the literature up to 1 June 2026, where it remains a stable record of the first phase of HEP-ML. A new resource, the HEP-ML Living Guide, replaces it. It does not list everything. It curates, it annotates, and it points readers to foundational and representative work, so that researchers can find their way into a mature and rapidly diversifying field. In this article we explain why we make this change and how the new resource works.

While relativistic effects alone lift degeneracies in ordinary hydrogen, providing indirect access to antimatter couplings, comparisons with antihydrogen measurements lead to significantly improved sensitivity to the antimatter couplings. In highly charged ions, enhanced relativistic effects further amplify the sensitivity, compensating for reduced experimental precision and larger theoretical uncertainties. We obtain the strongest bounds to date for scalar masses $m_φ\gtrsim 400\:\mathrm{keV}$. For comparison, we estimate astrophysical constraints on the same parameter space, providing strong bounds even on antimatter couplings, despite stars being predominantly composed of matter.

We demonstrate that these chains remain invariant under arbitrary local rephasings of the instantaneous matter eigenstates, thereby resolving the gauge dependence ambiguity typically associated with open path transport.

Abstract: We investigate small-instanton contributions to the axion potential across a range of KSVZ models containing vector-like quarks~(VQs), using naive dimensional analysis. The associated fermion zero-mode content varies between these cases, requiring different combinations of mass insertions and scalar--Yukawa contractions for its saturation. The resulting contribution then becomes sensitive to the UV completion and the induced potential can compete with, or dominate over, the ordinary QCD contribution. Assuming that the QCD and small-instanton potentials are aligned, we determine the resulting axion-mass shift and its consequences for the axion--photon coupling. When the small-instanton induced susceptibility becomes comparable to or larger than the QCD susceptibility, the physical axion mass of $m_a$ is enhanced at fixed decay constant~$f_a$, while the axion-photon of $g_{aγγ}$ coupling remains controlled by $f_a$ and the anomaly ratio of $E/N$. Abstract: We investigate whether squeezing can provide an observable signature of quantum gravitational waves. Because a realistic detector couples only to a particular wave-packet mode, squeezing in global source modes need not remain observable. We show that inflationary two-mode squeezing reduces to an unsqueezed thermal state in the accessible one-mode sector, phase incoherence washes out squeezing in stochastic backgrounds, and the limited coverage of the solid angle of detectors strongly suppresses squeezing from isolated sources. We then show that source squeezing is not essential, {\it i.e.}, a quantized gravitational wave can generate a positive squeezing witness if the detector state is initially prepared in a squeezed state, whereas a classical external gravitational field cannot, producing only a displacement. However, the resulting signal is bounded by the extremely small graviton--detector coupling. Thus, detector squeezing can remove the need for squeezed incident waves, but not the suppression caused by weak gravitational interaction.

This analysis evaluates different Bjorken-$x$ ($x_{\rm Bj}$) intervals across a range of $Q^2$ values. Abstract: We investigate the implications of brane-inspired modifications of the evolutionary history of the early universe on the process of baryogenesis via leptogenesis. A modified cosmic history alters the evolution of the Boltzmann equations governing lepton asymmetry, providing the possibility of successful leptogenesis in regions of the parameter space inaccessible in the standard cosmological scenario. Furthermore, a modified cosmic history also alters the shape of an otherwise scale-invariant spectral energy density (SED) of primary gravitational waves (PGWs) produced during inflation. This establishes a novel yet indirect probe of high scale leptogenesis scenarios through the observation of the SED of PGWs via future observations. We consider two scenarios in this work with single and multiple epochs of stiff equation of state(s) after inflation and before the epoch of radiation domination. We identify the parameter space where successful leptogenesis is possible, along with the possible observability of the PGWs, hence providing an indirect window into this leptogenesis scenario through PGWs.

Abstract: Large numerical-relativity surveys require compact-binary initial data that are both spectrally accurate and inexpensive to construct, including for systems with unequal masses and misaligned spins. We present \celephais, a compact-object initial-data code built on the \texttt{Kadath} spectral library, that constructs binary-neutron-star and black-hole--neutron-star initial data without imposing equatorial symmetry. The method exploits the sparse structure of the globally coupled multi-domain Jacobian and the approximate parity separation of fields. The assembled matrix is factored with \texttt{MUMPS} and reused as a refreshed right preconditioner in a Jacobian-free Newton--Krylov iteration, thereby avoiding dense storage. An adaptive $hp$--refinement scheme then concentrates resolution where the spectral tails are not yet resolved. For a mass-ratio-$20$ black-hole--neutron-star benchmark, the adaptive schemes recover the uniform-grid constraint accuracy with about three times fewer unknowns. We also extend the post-Newtonian force-balance estimate to arbitrary spin orientations and use it to initialise eccentricity reduction. Validation comprises binding-energy comparisons with post-Newtonian sequences, a precessing binary-neutron-star eccentricity-reduction test, and a full evolution whose waveform-reconstructed precession axis follows a post-Newtonian simple-precession model. These results establish an efficient route to spectrally resolved binary-neutron-star and black-hole--neutron-star initial data with arbitrary spin orientations.

Abstract: We report a multi-pipeline follow-up study of the continuous gravitational-wave (CW) outlier identified by the Ming et al. We can robustly recover the outlier in O3, while we find no evidence for a related standard CW signal in O4a (consistent with Ming et al.) and O4b data. We study how the shape of a surrounding dark matter (DM) halo modifies the ringdown and tidal response of an asymptotically flat, static, spherically symmetric BH. In the eikonal limit, the shift is governed by a single redshift integral encoding the halo mass distribution outside the light ring, explaining the close correspondence between the QNM frequencies, the light ring frequency, and the Lyapunov exponent. We also show that different combinations of compactness and profile shape can yield nearly degenerate ringdown spectra. Time-domain evolutions confirm the WKB frequencies and display the expected late-time Price law decay, with an intermediate tail controlled by the outer density falloff for slowly decaying profiles. Finally, we compute the static axial tidal Love number and show that it probes the halo with a radial weighting different from the ringdown sector. The combined ringdown and tidal response therefore provides a possible way to distinguish environmental effects from genuine deviations of the vacuum BH geometry.

Abstract: We present a numerical method that constructs geometrically defined coordinates on black-hole horizons, from which the multipole moments are computed without assuming axisymmetry. Abstract: In its standard black-hole realization, the Penrose process uses an event horizon to remove a negative-energy fragment. Abstract: Late-time wave tails decay at different rates along future null infinity and along timelike worldlines at finite radius. Abstract: The nonlinear gravitational-wave memory, or Christodoulou memory effect, is a permanent displacement produced by the self-interaction of gravitational waves predicted by General Relativity. This behaviour arises from the competition between increasing gravitational-wave luminosity and decreasing inspiral duration as the binary approaches the innermost stable circular orbit. Abstract: Parity violation in the gravitational and electromagnetic sectors has been extensively investigated, yet the two are conventionally treated as independent phenomena. Conclusion: 洛伦兹对称性破缺与拓扑缺陷协同调控黑洞强场引力现象，其效应可通过阴影、QNM、轨道进动等可观测量探测；eikonal–geodesic对应在Bumblebee–单极子背景下依然稳健；扩展热力学揭示：经典热力学量对洛伦兹破缺不敏感，但量子修正熵将宏观热机性能锚定于对称性破缺尺度，且热力学第二定律反向约束破缺参数幅值，体现量子引力效应与热力学基本原理的深层统一。

Abstract: We investigate the geodesic dynamics, eikonal quasinormal modes (QNMs), and extended phase space thermodynamics of a Schwarzschild-de Sitter-like black hole coupled with a global monopole in Bumblebee gravity. Our analysis reveals that spontaneous Lorentz symmetry breaking and topological defects modify the effective radial dynamics, leading to lower effective potential barriers and a reduced magnitude of the effective force. For null geodesics, higher monopole parameters expand the photon sphere and critical impact parameter, implying an enlarged black hole shadow, while mitigating dynamical instability. For massive particles, the stable circular orbit window narrows significantly as the Outermost Stable Circular Orbit (OSCO) shrinks, alongside a marked enhancement in perihelion precession. In addition, we examine the correspondence between the eikonal QNMs and the properties of null geodesics. The excellent agreement between the eikonal approximation and the WKB method for scalar and electromagnetic perturbations supports the validity of the correspondence for the spacetime under consideration. In the extended thermodynamic phase space, we evaluate the black hole as a holographic heat engine. We demonstrate that classical efficiency is mathematically blind to Lorentz violation; however, introducing a quantum-corrected modified entropy tightly couples the macroscopic work output to the symmetry-breaking framework. Ultimately, the necessity to respect the Carnot bound imposes a strict macroscopic constraint on the Lorentz-violating parameter, safeguarding the Second Law of Thermodynamics.

</details>


### [97] [Observational constraints on fractional holographic dark energy in the light of DESI DR2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09379)
*Qihong Huang,Yuchen Zhang,Bing Xu,Kaituo Zhang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建三种分数全息暗能量模型（FHDEH、FHDEF、FHDEP），采用贝叶斯统计方法联合SN Ia（Pantheon+）、OHD、DESI DR2 BAO数据进行参数估计，并辅以CMB距离先验（R、lₐ）进行模型比对（AIC/BIC）及动力学分析（Ωₘ(z)、Ω_de(z)、q(z)）。

Result: FHDEH/FHDEP/FHDEF三模型χ²_min均略低于ΛCDM，但AIC/BIC未显示统计显著性；加入CMB先验后FHDEH与FHDEP被强烈排除（ΔBIC > 10）；仅FHDEF保持与ΛCDM相近的Ωₘ与Ω_de红移演化，但其减速参量q(z)在z < 0时系统偏离ΛCDM，呈现非标准晚期加速行为。

Conclusion: FHDEF是唯一在当前观测下自洽且具备物理可行性的分数全息暗能量实现，其晚期q(z)偏离揭示了分数熵结构可能蕴含超越ΛCDM的动力学自由度，为检验量子引力启发的暗能量机制提供了可观测窗口。

Abstract: Based on the fractional entropy from fractional quantum mechanics, fractional holographic dark energy (FHDE) has been proposed with the Hubble horizon as the IR cutoff (FHDEH). We extend this framework by adopting the future event horizon and the particle horizon as the IR cutoff, proposing the FHDEF and FHDEP models. Using the SN+OHD+DESI DR2 dataset to constrain these models, we find that all three models provide a marginally lower $χ^{2}_{min}$ compared to $Λ$CDM but without significant preference according to AIC and BIC. When CMB distance priors are included, the FHDEH and FHDEP models are strongly ruled out. We further analyze the cosmological evolution for these models, and find that only the FHDEF model predicts nearly identical evolutions of $Ω_{m}$ and $Ω_{de}$ to those of the $Λ$CDM model across cosmic history, but its deceleration parameter $q$ deviate from the $Λ$CDM model in the future, indicating richer late time dynamics beyond the standard $Λ$CDM cosmology.

</details>


### [98] [Dynamical Barbero--Immirzi field coupled to quintessence: gravitational-wave propagation constraints and next-generation forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09487)
*Zhi-Fu Gao,Hui Wang,Luiz Carlos Garcia de Andrade,Na Wang,Guo-Qiang Jin,Zhou-Jian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了动力学Barbero-Immirzi (BI)场γ(x)与quintessence标量场φ耦合对引力波传播的影响，在Einstein-Cartan-Holst引力框架下，发现该耦合引入新的摩擦项，导致GW传播方程修正；首次利用GWTC-3暗警报数据同时约束两个新参数ξBI和ξcp，并给出ET/CE未来观测的灵敏度预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力效应（通过动力学Barbero-Immirzi场体现）与暗能量（quintessence）之间的可能联系，借助引力波传播作为高精度探针检验LQG相关 phenomenology。

Method: 在Einstein-Cartan-Holst理论中将BI参数提升为动力学标量场γ(x)，引入最小耦合项βφ²γ²；推导含耦合的修正引力波传播方程；结合GWTC-3暗警报Ξ₀测量，进行贝叶斯参数估计；并基于ET/CE噪声曲线开展 Fisher 信息矩阵预报。

Result: 首次获得ξBI ≲ 0.7 与 ξcp ≲ 0.13（90%置信度）的同时约束；预报ET/CE十年观测可将精度提升至σ(ξBI) ∼ 3×10⁻²、σ(ξcp) ∼ 1.2×10⁻²；对应微观参数约束γ_dyn ≲ 10⁻¹²、β ≲ 10⁻³。

Conclusion: 引力波传播是探测动力学Barbero-Immirzi场及其与暗能量耦合的有效新通道，为圈量子引力与修改引力理论提供可观测判据。

Abstract: We investigate the imprints of a dynamical Barbero--Immirzi (BI) field $γ(x)$ coupled to a quintessence scalar field $φ$ on gravitational-wave (GW) propagation. In the framework of Einstein--Cartan--Holst gravity, promoting $γ$ to a dynamical scalar introduces a stress--energy that back-reacts on the metric, modifying the GW friction term. A minimal coupling $\proptoβ\,φ^2γ^2$ between the BI field and quintessence leads to a two-parameter extension of the Belgacem--Maggiore parametrization, characterized by $\xBI$ (from the isolated BI field) and $\xcp$ (from the coupling). This is the first study to simultaneously constrain both parameters using GW data. We derive the modified GW propagation equation in the coupled system and identify a distinctive redshift dependence: the coupling-induced term grows faster than the isolated BI term, offering a handle to break degeneracies. Using the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA GWTC-3 dark-siren constraint $Ξ_0=1.2^{+0.7}_{-0.7}$, we obtain the first simultaneous constraints: $|\xBI|\lesssim0.7$ and $|\xcp|\lesssim0.13$ at 90\% credibility. We then forecast the sensitivity of next-generation detectors Einstein Telescope (ET) and Cosmic Explorer (CE), showing that a 10-year observation campaign can improve these bounds by two orders of magnitude, reaching $σ(\xBI)\sim3\times10^{-2}$ and $σ(\xcp)\sim1.2\times10^{-2}$. Translated into microscopic parameters, this corresponds to $γ_{\rm dyn}\lesssim10^{-12}$ and $β\lesssim10^{-3}$, providing a powerful new observational window into the interplay between quantum-gravity phenomenology and dark energy. Our results demonstrate that GW propagation offers a promising avenue to probe the dynamical BI field and its coupling to the dark sector, with implications for loop quantum gravity and modified gravity theories.

</details>


### [99] [Low-energy limit in the anomaly-induced action and the semiclassical cosmological bounce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09720)
*Wagno Cesar e Silva,Nicolas R. Bertini,Ilya L. Shapiro*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非空收缩宇宙的宇宙反弹模型，其中物质在接近奇点时热化为辐射，其正beta函数结合迹反常自动触发反弹；文章发展了适用于原初宇宙扰动研究的低能有效作用量形式，通过引入多个辅助标量场避免高阶导数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索由迹反常驱动的宇宙学反弹机制，并为其原初扰动分析建立可计算的有效理论框架。

Method: 构建低能极限下的非局域有效作用量，利用多个辅助标量场实现局域化，规避高阶导数带来的不稳定性问题。

Result: 得到了一个不含高阶时间导数的、由多个辅助标量描述的局域化低能有效作用量形式，为后续计算原初扰动谱奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该形式主义为研究迹反常驱动反弹模型中的标量和张量扰动提供了自洽且稳定的理论工具，具有可观测宇宙学意义。

Abstract: In the recently proposed scenario, the cosmological bounce occurs because the initially contracting Universe is not empty. In the region close to singularity, matter contents of the Universe heat up and effectively become radiation. Then, the trace anomaly automatically provides bounce if the overall beta function in the matter sector is positive. Independent of the remaining open questions on the quantum field theory side, it is interesting to consider this model from the cosmological perspective. In the present work, we develop the general formalism which is a necessary step for exploring the primordial cosmological perturbations. The main technical development is the formulation of the low-energy version for the nonlocal part of the effective action. The complete form of this action can be done local using two auxiliary scalars. In our new version, there are more scalars, but this enables one to avoid higher derivatives.

</details>


### [100] [Formation of trapped surfaces for the spherically symmetric Einstein-Yang-Mills system with non-trivial incoming data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09746)
*Nikolaos Athanasiou,Puskar Mondal,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了球对称爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯方程在双零规范下的陷俘面形成定理，推广了An-Lim的奇异特征方法至非阿贝尔杨-米尔斯场，为弱宇宙监督猜想提供关键一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为证明爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯系统的弱宇宙监督猜想（Weak Cosmic Censorship）奠定基础，需首先建立其动力学中陷俘面形成的严格数学判据。

Method: 在双零坐标系下构造球对称解，采用奇异特征初值问题方法（singular characteristic method），处理非阿贝尔规范场导致的曲率耦合与非线性项，克服杨-米尔斯场非交换性带来的新结构困难。

Result: 首次证明在允许非平凡入射数据的双零特征初值条件下，球对称爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯系统存在陷俘面形成；该结果是弱宇宙监督猜想研究的第一步。

Conclusion: 该定理表明，即使在非阿贝尔规范场强非线性与时空曲率强耦合情形下，引力坍缩仍可触发黑洞视界前兆——陷俘面的形成，支持弱宇宙监督猜想的普适性。

Abstract: We establish a trapped surface formation theorem for the spherically symmetric Einstein Yang Mills equations in a double-null gauge. The theorem concerns characteristic initial data posed on a pair of transversely intersecting null hypersurfaces and allows nontrivial incoming data. The proof extends the singular characteristic method of An and Lim for the Einstein Maxwell Charged Scalar Field System to the non-abelian Yang Mills setting, where the curvature coupling and gauge field nonlinearities introduce new structural difficulties. This paper constitutes the first part of a program toward weak cosmic censorship for the Einstein Yang Mills system.

</details>


### [101] [Adiabatic Perturbations in GW170817-Compatible Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09783)

Method: 基于EGB暴胀背景动力学，推导标量与张量扰动的有效作用量，计算绝热条件（即慢变参数时间导数远小于哈勃参量），并结合CMB功率谱与GW170817对张量传播速度c_T=1的强约束，分析两类可行模型中绝热性是否被破坏及其发生时刻。

Result: 绝热性要求显著约束EGB耦合函数及其导数；第一类模型全程满足绝热近似，第二类仅在暴胀末期短暂违反；后者导致扰动模混合与功率谱畸变，且仅第一类模型同时满足CMB、GW170817与绝热性三重约束。

Conclusion: 在所有与CMB和GW170817兼容的EGB暴胀模型中，仅全程保持绝热性的那一类模型无物理病态，表明绝热性是筛选高阶引力暴胀理论的关键判据。

Abstract: We study the adiabaticity of the cosmological perturbations in the context of inflationary Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet theories. We focus on viable inflationary Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet theories which are compatible with the current Cosmic Microwave Background radiation experiments and also are compatible with the GW170817 observations. We derive the effects of the adiabaticity requirement on the Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet physical parameters and we show that the sound speed of the scalar perturbations and the propagation speed of the tensor perturbations are constrained. We consider two classes of inflationary viable and GW170817-compatible theories, and in the first class the adiabaticity is not violated during inflation, while in the second class the adiabaticity is violated only at the end of inflation. We discuss the effects of the adiabaticity violation in the second class of models. From our analysis, it seems that only one class of viable EGB inflationary theories, which is also compatible with the GW170817 event, is free from adiabaticity pathologies.

</details>


### [102] [Probing ultralight bosons with LISA observations of spinning black hole mergers and follow-up searches of merger remnants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09811)
*Ifigeneia Giannakoudi,Maxence Corman,William E. East*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻玻色子是超出标准模型的新物理候选者，其存在可通过黑洞超辐射机制产生可观测引力波信号；当前缺乏对其质量范围的有效观测约束，亟需利用LISA等新一代引力波天文台开展系统性探测。

Result: 自旋测量对标量玻色子质量约束达5×10⁻¹⁸–10⁻¹⁴ eV，矢量玻色子达6×10⁻¹⁹–2×10⁻¹⁴ eV；后续引力波搜寻仅对矢量玻色子在~3×10⁻¹⁷–3×10⁻¹⁵ eV区间具显著灵敏度，且在10⁻¹⁶–2×10⁻¹⁵ eV区间探测概率随模型从极低至接近100%变化。

Conclusion: LISA有望通过双黑洞自旋统计与残余黑洞后续引力波搜寻协同探测超轻玻色子；自旋测量提供宽质量范围排除能力，而后续搜寻在特定矢量玻色子质量窗内具备高信噪比探测潜力，二者构成互补探针。

Abstract: Ultralight bosons can trigger superradiant instabilities around
  rotating black holes, extracting angular momentum and leading in some cases to observable gravitational signatures.
  When the associated spin-down timescale is shorter than the black hole lifetimes and the spin-up timescales due to, e.g. accretion, this process imposes an upper limit on black hole spins. In addition, the formation and subsequent dissipation of boson clouds generates quasi-continuous gravitational wave emission. In this work, we explore the prospects for constraining and detecting ultralight bosons with observations of massive black hole binary mergers with the space-based LISA observatory. We consider two complementary approaches: measurements of black hole spins from merging binaries and follow-up gravitational wave searches targeting massive black hole binary merger remnants. We consider three population models for massive black holes, based on either heavy or light seeds,
  and forecast the exclusion and detection probabilities for both scalar and vector bosons.
  We find that black hole spin measurements can constrain scalar masses in the range $[5\times10^{-18},10^{-14}]$ eV and vector masses in the range $[6\times10^{-19},2\times10^{-14}]$ eV, with the exact range depending on the model.
  In contrast, restricting to vector bosons, follow-up gravitational wave searches are sensitive to a narrower vector boson mass range of $\sim[3\times10^{-17},3\times10^{-15}]$ eV, with the specific values again depending on the model. If a vector boson with a mass in the range $[10^{-16},2\times10^{-15}]$ eV existed, the probability of having an event that the follow-up searches would be sensitive to ranges from very small to near unity depending on the astrophysical model.

</details>


### [103] [Rotating near-horizon extreme geometries in quadratic gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09847)
*Breno L. Giacchini,Ivan Kolář*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了二次引力理论中旋转近视界极端解，通过幂级数展开与数值计算相结合的方法，在AdS₂结构、球形视界拓扑和赤道反射对称性假设下，利用共形坐标简化Einstein–Weyl引力场方程；运用Frobenius方法分类赤道与极点附近的幂级数解并导出递推关系；数值上精细调节自由参数以满足全局正则性约束；发现部分Bachian分支存在显著视界形变，并暗示存在对应极端旋转黑洞尺寸的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高阶引力修正（特别是二次引力）下极端黑洞近视界几何的可能结构，检验其正则性、存在性及物理可实现性，尤其关注Einstein–Weyl引力（零标量曲率的二次引力）中Bach张量主导的动力学效应。

Conclusion: 在Einstein–Weyl引力框架下，旋转近视界极端几何受Bach张量强约束；正则解的存在具有非平凡参数依赖性；视界面积存在上界，为高阶引力中极端黑洞的普适性尺度限制提供了新证据。

Abstract: Rotating near-horizon extreme solutions of quadratic gravity are analyzed combining power series expansions and numerical calculations. Restricting to geometries with symmetries of the near-horizon extreme Kerr black hole (i.e., with the $\mathrm{AdS_2}$-structure), spherical horizon topology, and equatorial reflection symmetry, we introduce conformal coordinates simplifying the field equations of Einstein--Weyl gravity, i.e., quadratic gravity with vanishing scalar curvature. Employing the Frobenius analysis, we classify all power series solutions expanded around the equator as well as the poles, and obtain the recurrence relations. With the help of numerical analysis, we study fine-tuning of the free parameter to satisfy the global constraints on regular near-horizon extreme geometries. Computations of the horizon area, horizon scalar curvature, and rotational scalar reveal strong horizon deformations in some Bachian branches. Moreover, the absence of regular Bachian near-horizon geometries above a finite horizon area suggests an upper bound on the size of the corresponding extremal rotating black holes.

</details>

### [104] [Analytic Integration of the Lambert W Cosmic Fluid Model H(z) Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07878)
*Ken Roberts,S. R. Valluri*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了Lambert W宇宙流体状态方程模型中相对哈勃参数 $ H(z)/H_0 $ 的解析表达式，替代了Dubey等人（2026）工作中依赖数值积分的公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dubey等人的工作采用数值积分计算 $ \ln(H(z)/H_0) $，而解析解可提升计算效率、便于参数空间探索及理论分析。

Method: 利用Lambert W函数的积分恒等式与状态方程 $ p = w(\rho)\rho $ 对应的弗里德曼方程，对能量守恒方程进行变量替换与解析积分。

Result: 得到了 $ H(z)/H_0 $ 的闭合形式解析解，显式依赖于Lambert W函数及其参数，避免了数值积分误差与计算开销。

Conclusion: 该解析解为Lambert W宇宙学模型提供了更高效、鲁棒的观测拟合与理论研究工具，强化了其作为暗能量候选模型的可行性。

Abstract: The Lambert $W$ (LW) model for the cosmic fluid equation of state was proposed by S. Saha and K. Bamba in 2019-2020. A recent (early 2026) paper by Dubey, et al, carries out a new fit of the LW model to observational data, in order to estimate the model's parameters. That paper exhibits a formula for the logarithm of the relative Hubble factor at redshift $z$, $\ln(H(z)/H_0)$, which is based upon a numerical integration. That integral can be evaluated analytically, and we present the details in this working paper. The resulting analytic expression for $H(z)/H_0$ may be convenient for exploration of the LW model.

</details>


### [105] [Perturbation Theory for Time-dependent Singular Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08108)
*Kaya Güven Akbaş,Fatih Erman,O. Teoman Turgut*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个针对具有纯离散未扰动谱的量子系统中时变点相互作用的微扰理论框架，通过热核正则化处理二维和三维情形下的发散问题，而一维情形无需重整化；进而展开非自治哈密顿量并计算了能级移动、投影修正与跃迁振幅，并以多个具体模型验证该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展适用于时变点相互作用（如运动δ势或时变耦合强度）的严格微扰理论，弥补现有方法在连续谱或非静态情形下的局限性，并统一处理不同维度下正则化需求的差异。

Method: 基于静态点相互作用问题的本征谱展开非自治哈密顿量；对二维和三维情形采用热核正则化构造重整化预解式，一维情形直接使用有限的对角格林函数；系统计算一阶与二阶极点位移、投影修正项及跃迁振幅。

Result: 获得时变点相互作用下能级的微扰修正（含实部位移与虚部衰减）、态投影的修正项，以及不同本征态间的跃迁概率幅；在多个可解模型（如时变耦合δ势、球面上运动δ势、受运动δ势扰动的谐振子）中给出显式结果；一维情形退化为标准含时微扰论。

Conclusion: 该框架为离散谱量子系统中时变点相互作用提供了统一、可计算且数学上自洽的微扰方法，兼具物理直观性与形式严谨性，适用于量子控制、瞬态输运及非平衡量子动力学等场景。

Abstract: We develop a perturbative framework for time-dependent point interactions in quantum systems with purely discrete unperturbed spectrum. In two and three dimensions the point interaction is described through the renormalized resolvent obtained from heat-kernel regularization, while in one dimension the diagonal Green function is finite and no renormalization is required. Time dependence is introduced either through the interaction parameter or through the motion of the support point. In both cases we expand the non-autonomous Hamiltonian in the spectral basis of the corresponding static point-interaction problem and derive the first- and second-order pole shifts, projection corrections, and transition amplitudes. The method is illustrated by explicit examples: point interactions with time-dependent coupling, harmonic oscillators perturbed by moving point interactions, and a particle on a sphere with a moving interaction center. We also discuss the one-dimensional harmonic oscillator with a moving delta potential, showing that in the non-renormalized case the present formulation reduces to the standard time-dependent perturbation theory.

</details>

*Peter J. Olver,Francis Valiquette*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的等变活动标架法的递归实现，用于无穷维李伪群作用，通过在计算延拓作用前执行伪群规范化，避免了中间表达式过度复杂化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免在计算延拓作用过程中引入不必要的复杂中间表达式，提升等变活动标架法在无穷维李伪群作用下的计算效率与可操作性。

Method: 基于等变活动标架理论，设计递归算法，在伪群作用延拓之前完成规范化步骤，结合微分不变量与符号计算技术。

Result: 实现了更简洁高效的伪群规范化流程，并通过多个具体例子验证了该算法的有效性与普适性。

Conclusion: 该递归实现显著简化了无穷维李伪群作用下等变活动标架的构造过程，为微分不变量理论及几何化建模提供了新工具。

Abstract: A new recursive implementation of the equivariant moving frame method for infinite-dimensional Lie pseudo-group actions is presented. It allows pseudo-group normalizations to be performed before the prolonged action is computed, thereby avoiding the introduction of unnecessarily complicated intermediate expressions. Several examples are provided to illustrate the algorithm.

</details>


### [107] [New results on the domain of analyticity of the free energy for the Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08396)
*Nguyen Tong Xuan,Nguyen Dang Thien Thu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Fernandez–Procacci判据、生成函数技术和图论方法，改进了Ising模型在非零外磁场下高温、低温及强场区域自由能解析性的收敛条件，尤其在强场与低温区获得更优的聚合物/轮廓估计，并借助Veblen定理构造的新高温展开显著扩大了解析区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定Ising模型在非零外磁场下自由能的解析性区域，弥补经典结果在高温和强场/低温区收敛半径不足的问题。

Method: 结合Fernandez–Procacci簇展开收敛判据、生成函数技术（用于聚合物与轮廓计数）以及图论方法（特别是基于Veblen定理的高阶环分解），对三种物理区域分别进行严格估计。

Result: 在高温区构造出基于Veblen定理的新展开式，大幅扩展自由能的解析区域；在强场与低温区，生成函数方法给出比传统Peierls或Dobrushin方法更锐利的轮廓聚合物界；整体上统一并改进了三类参数区域的解析性阈值。

Conclusion: Ising模型自由能在非零磁场下的解析性可通过现代组合分析与簇展开理论得到系统性增强，其收敛域远超经典Dobrushin–Kotecký–Shlosman或Lee–Yang框架所能覆盖的范围。

Abstract: We investigate the analyticity of the free energy of the Ising model in the presence of a non-zero external magnetic field, at high temperature, and at low temperature. Using the Fernandez--Procacci convergence criterion for cluster expansions, together with generating-function techniques and graph-theoretical methods, we derive improved convergence conditions in all three regimes. In particular, the generating-function approach yields sharper estimates for polymers and contours in the strong-field and low-temperature regimes, while a new high-temperature expansion based on Veblen's theorem provides a substantially larger analyticity region than the classical results in the literature.

</details>


### [108] [Notes on the Optical-Lattice Hard-Core Bose Gas of Aizenman--Lieb--Seiringer--Solovej--Yngvason](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08409)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是对Aizenman等人关于交错光晶格硬核玻色气体研究的详细阐述性笔记，旨在统一符号、明确隐含步骤，并阐释热力学极限下平衡态的中心分解与轨道分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使原始论文中压缩或委托处理的步骤显式化，便于读者理解。

Method: 采用统一符号重述并扩展原始论证，系统梳理关键输入，并分析平衡态的中心分解（central decomposition）与轨道分解（orbitwise decomposition）。

Result: 清晰呈现了交错光晶格硬核玻色气体在热力学极限下平衡态的结构，特别是其在遍历分量（ergodic components）上的分解性质。

Conclusion: 该笔记未提出新方法或改进估计，而是通过概念澄清与技术细节展开，增强了原始结果的可读性与教学价值。

Abstract: These are detailed expository notes on the staggered optical-lattice hard-core Bose gas of Aizenman, Lieb, Seiringer, Solovej and Yngvason. They restate and expand the arguments and their principal inputs in a common notation, and describe the central and orbitwise decompositions of thermodynamic-limit equilibrium states. The purpose is to make compressed or delegated steps explicit, not to introduce a new method, strengthen the estimates, or otherwise improve the cited results.

</details>


### [109] [The $\mathcal{L}$-Calculus for Causal Variational Principles: An Exterior Differential Calculus on Non-Smooth Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08811)
*Felix Finster,Niky Kamran,Frank van der Top*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种用于因果变分原理的微分演算，将外微分形式的外微分、de Rham上同调、粘接构造（如限制与延拓、Mayer-Vietoris序列）、Künneth公式及Poincaré引理等推广至非光滑空间，并证明了Stokes定理和Gauss散度定理的相应版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在非光滑时空结构（如因果集或离散几何）中建立可微结构与拓扑不变量的严格数学框架，以支撑量子引力与因果变分原理的数学基础。

Method: 基于因果变分原理所定义的非光滑测度空间，构建广义微分形式代数，引入适配于因果结构的外微分算子与上同调理论，并通过局部-整体技术（如层论与粘接引理）确立全局定理。

Result: 建立了适用于因果变分原理框架的广义微分演算体系，包括外微分、de Rham上同调、Mayer-Vietoris序列、Künneth公式、Poincaré引理、Stokes定理与Gauss散度定理的非光滑版本。

Conclusion: 该微分演算成功将经典微分拓扑的核心结构推广至非光滑因果空间，为基于因果结构的量子场论与背景无关引力理论提供了严格的分析工具。

Abstract: A differential calculus for causal variational principles is developed, which generalizes the exterior calculus of differential forms and some of the associated differential topological structures to non-smooth spaces. Our calculus includes the exterior derivative, de Rham cohomology, glueing constructions (restrictions and extensions of differential forms, Mayer-Vietoris sequence), a Künneth formula and Poincar{é}'s lemma. Moreover, we prove versions of Stokes' theorem and the Gauß divergence theorem. The constructions and results are illustrated by several examples.

</details>


### [110] [Theoretical analysis of the maximum range of a projectile released from a pendulum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09157)
*Ken Yamamoto*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了从单摆释放抛体的运动，重点研究了使水平射程最大的最优释放角及其对应的最大射程；该系统是‘泰山跳跃’问题的简化模型；通过解析方法得出最优释放角满足一个三次方程，且随初速度增大而增大；并推导出低速与高速极限下最优角和最大射程的渐近表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立‘泰山跳跃’问题的简化物理模型，探究在单摆末端释放抛体时实现最大水平射程的最优动力学条件。

Method: 基于经典力学中的能量守恒与抛体运动学，结合单摆约束运动与自由抛体运动的衔接条件，推导射程关于释放角与初速度的函数，并通过极值分析导出最优释放角所满足的三次方程；进一步采用渐近分析方法处理低速与高速极限情形。

Result: 最优释放角θ_opt由一含初速度v₀的三次代数方程确定；θ_opt随v₀单调递增；当v₀→0时，θ_opt ∼ π/2 − (2/3)v₀² + O(v₀⁴)，最大射程R_max ∼ (2/3)v₀³；当v₀→∞时，θ_opt → π/2，R_max ∼ v₀²/g。

Conclusion: 单摆释放抛体的最大射程问题具有非平凡的几何-动力学耦合特征：最优释放角并非固定值，而是初速度的函数，其行为由三次方程刻画，并在高低速极限下呈现可解析的渐近规律；该结果深化了对约束释放类抛体问题最优控制的理解。

Abstract: The motion of a projectile released from a simple pendulum is analyzed, with particular emphasis on investigating the optimal release angle that maximizes the horizontal range and the corresponding maximum range. This system serves as a simplified model of the Tarzan jump problem. Using simple analytical methods, the optimal release angle is shown to be characterized by a cubic equation and to increase with the initial velocity. In addition, asymptotic expressions for both the optimal angle and maximum range are derived in the limits of low and high initial velocity.

</details>


### [111] [Eigenvalue asymptotics for the one-particle density matrix and one-particle kinetic energy density operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09570)
*Søren Fournais,Alexander V. Sobolev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究N粒子原子薛定谔算子本征函数ψ对应的单粒子密度矩阵γ(x,y)和单粒子动能密度κ(x,y)的积分算子Γ与K的谱渐近行为，揭示其本征值衰减速率由ψ在粒子对合点处的奇异性决定，并利用Birman-Solomyak理论及ψ的正则性结果导出精确幂律渐近公式；对全反对称ψ，因Cusp条件导致更高阶正则性，本征值衰减更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量化量子化学中有限维近似（如Hartree-Fock或DFT）的截断误差，需精确刻画单粒子密度矩阵Γ与动能密度算子K的本征值渐近分布，而该分布本质上依赖于多体波函数ψ在粒子对合点处的奇性结构。

Method: 结合多体波函数的Cusp条件与近期建立的正则性理论（如Kato cusp condition及H^2_{loc}正则性），分离并刻画ψ在双粒子合点处的奇异性；再通过Birman-Solomyak关于齐次符号伪微分算子的谱渐近定理，将γ与κ的奇异积分核映射至相应伪微分算子，从而导出本征值λ_k(Γ)与λ_k(K)的精确代数衰减率。

Result: 对一般本征函数ψ：λ_k(Γ) ∼ A^{8/3} k^{-8/3}, λ_k(K) ∼ B^2 k^{-2}；对全反对称ψ：λ_k(Γ) ∼ (A_asym)^{10/3} k^{-10/3}, λ_k(K) ∼ (B_asym)^{8/3} k^{-8/3}；其中常数A,B,A_asym,B_asym均由ψ及其梯度在合点处的极限行为显式给出。

Conclusion: 单粒子算子的谱渐近完全由多体波函数在双粒子合点处的奇异性主导；全反对称性通过提升ψ的局部正则性（如∇ψ的L^∞有界性），显著加速Γ与K本征值的衰减，为量子化学基组截断误差提供最优代数标度。

Abstract: Let $ψ({\mathbf x})$, ${\mathbf x} \in{\mathbb R}^{3N}$, be an eigenfunction of the $N$-particle atomic Schrödinger operator. We consider the one-particle density matrix $γ(x, y)$ and one-particle kinetic energy density $\varkappa(x, y)$, $x, y\in {\mathbb R}^3$, associated with the eigenfunction $ψ$. Both functions play a central role in quantum chemistry computations of atomic and molecular bound states: the knowledge of the eigenvalue behaviour of the integral operators ${\sfΓ}$ and ${\sf{K}}$ with kernels $γ(x, y)$ and $\varkappa(x, y)$ serves to estimate the errors due to finite-dimensional approximations. We find the following asymptotic formulas for their eigenvalues $λ_k({\sfΓ})>0$ and $λ_k({\sf{K}})>0$: \[
  \lim_{k\to \infty} k^{\frac{8}{3}} \,λ_k({\sfΓ}) = A^{\frac{8}{3}},\quad \lim_{k\to \infty} k^2\,λ_k({\sf{K}}) = B^2, \] where $A$ and $B$ are non-negative constants given explicitly in terms of the eigenfunction $ψ$. These asymptotics are determined by the singularities of the function $ψ$ at pair coalescence points of the particles. To identify and isolate these singularities we use some recent regularity results for $ψ$. At the last step we apply Birman-Solomyak spectral asymptotics results for pseudodifferential operators with homogeneous symbols.
  In the special case where the eigenfunction $ψ$ is totally antisymmetric, it exhibits enhanced regularity, which leads to a faster decay of the eigenvalues $λ_k(\sfΓ)$ and $λ_k(\sf{K})$. The asymptotic formulas take the form \[ \lim_{k\to \infty} k^{\frac{10}{3}} \,λ_k({\sfΓ}) = \big(A_{asym}\big)^{\frac{10}{3}},\quad \lim_{k\to \infty} k^{\frac{8}{3}} \,λ_k({\sf K}) = \big(B_{asym}\big)^{\frac{8}{3}}, \] where $A_{asym}$ and $B_{asym}$ are non-negative constants given explicitly in terms of the gradient of $ψ$.

</details>


### [112] [The determinant of the Dirichlet-to-Neumann map for a surface with boundary and periods of holomorphic differentials on its double](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09737)
*Dmitrii Korikov,Alexey Kokotov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了具有连通边界的2d黎曼曲面M上Dirichlet-to-Neumann算子ζ-行列式的共形不变量的初等表达式，仅依赖于其对称延拓2M上的全纯微分周期，结合Hilbert变换与Kontsevich-Vishik型伪微分算子行列式正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为替代Guillarmou-Guillopé基于负曲率双曲几何（Ruelle/Selberg zeta函数）的复杂表达式，寻求一个仅依赖复几何结构（即双曲面2M上的全纯微分周期）的初等、内在且共形不变的公式。

Method: 利用边界Hilbert变换的谱性质、曲面M的对称延拓2M的复结构、其上全纯微分的周期矩阵，以及Kontsevich-Vishik-Friedlander-Guillemin对伪微分算子行列式的zeta函数正则化框架；建立长度谱与周期之间的显式联系。

Result: 给出了det_ζ(Λ)/|Γ|的显式初等公式，完全由双曲面2M的全纯微分周期决定；同时建立了（共形化后）M、2M的长度谱与2M上全纯微分周期之间的精确关系。

Conclusion: Dirichlet-to-Neumann算子的共形不变行列式可完全由双曲对称延拓2M的复几何数据（全纯微分周期）刻画，无需引入无穷体积双曲模型或zeta函数，从而统一并简化了高亏格情形下的共形不变量描述。

Abstract: Let $(M,g)$ be a smooth orientable $2d$ Riemannian manifold of genus $\mathfrak{g}$ with Riemannian metric $g$ and connected boundary $Γ$. Let $Λ$ be the Dirichlet-to-Neumann map on $Γ$ and let ${\rm det}_ζ(Λ)$ be its (modified, i. e. with zero mode excluded) $ζ$-regularized determinant. It is well-known that the quantity ${\rm det}_ζ(Λ)/|Γ|$ (where $|Γ|$ is the length of $Γ$) is a conformal invariant. It was shown by Edward and Wu (\cite{EV}) that this invariant equals one for $\mathfrak{g}=0$; in the case $\mathfrak{g}>0$ Guillarmou and Guillopé \cite{Guillarmou} found two explicit expressions for this invariant through the Rouelle and (respectively) the Selberg zeta-functions of the two surfaces of negative constant curvature from the conformal class of $(M,g)$: one is of infinite volume and complete whereas another has geodesic boundary.
  We present an elementary counterpart of the formulae of Guillarmou and Guillopé using the periods of holomorphic differentials on the double $2M$ of $M$ only. Our approach is based on the properties of the Hilbert transform of $M$ \cite{B,HilbKor} and the Kontsevich-Vishik-Friedlander-Guillemin regularization of the determinants of pseudodifferenial operators \cite{KV,Ww,F}. In particular, a connection between the length spectra of (uniformized) $M$, $2M$ and the periods of holomorphic differentials on $2M$ is established.

</details>

### [113] [SuperEM: A Sub-meV Threshold Detector Architecture for Cosmic Neutrino Background and Dark Matter Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08592)
*Zhenjie Li,Xilei Sun,Xiaoshan Jiang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型超导-半导体耦合电子倍增（SuperEM）探测器，旨在突破现有辐射探测技术中亚毫电子伏阈值、时间响应与空间可扩展性之间的‘不可能三角’限制，为宇宙中微子背景（CνB）直接探测、绝对中微子质量标度测定及亚GeV轻暗物质（LDM）搜寻提供兼具亚meV能量阈值、高绝对能量分辨率、纳秒级时间响应和大规模可扩展性的新探测范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破当前辐射探测器在亚meV能量阈值、宏观时间响应与空间可扩展性之间存在的根本性权衡（即‘不可能三角’），以支撑CνB直接探测、绝对中微子质量测量及亚GeV轻暗物质搜寻等前沿物理目标。

Method: 提出SuperEM探测器架构：将超导吸收体（实现亚meV阈值）与强偏置高密度半导体PN结（实现本征高增益数字化）耦合；利用非平衡准粒子产生、量子隧穿穿越ALD超薄绝缘势垒完成信号读出；结合深低温雪崩机制实证基础，开展信号输运仿真验证纳秒级电子漂移动力学。

Result: 理论建模与输运仿真表明：无掺杂界面配合强漂移场可实现高效、纳秒尺度瞬态电子漂移，全信号响应时间≤35 ns；该架构在保持亚meV探测阈值的同时，兼顾高能量分辨率、快时间响应与大规模可扩展性。

Conclusion: SuperEM代表一种原理性突破的探测器新范式，为下一代CνB与LDM实验提供了兼具超高灵敏度、时间精度与工程可扩展性的技术路径。

Abstract: Expanding the operational boundaries of radiation detection is imperative for contemporary particle physics, astrophysics, and cosmology. At this frontier, the direct detection of the Cosmic Neutrino Background (C$ν$B), the determination of the absolute neutrino mass scale, and the search for sub-GeV Light Dark Matter (LDM) necessitate detector architectures capable of sub-millielectronvolt (sub-meV) energy thresholds, exceptional absolute energy resolution, fast time response, and massive scalability. Current technologies confront an intrinsic limit---the ``impossible triangle''---wherein optimizing for sub-meV thresholds inherently compromises either macroscopic timing response or spatial scalability. Here, we introduce the Superconductor-Coupled Semiconductor Electron-Multiplying (SuperEM) detector, a fundamentally novel structural paradigm designed to bypass this limitation. The architecture couples the ultra-low energy threshold of a superconducting absorber with the intrinsic high-gain digitization of a strongly biased, high-density semiconductor P-N junction. Incident energy yields a proliferation of non-equilibrium quasiparticles, which are subsequently extracted via quantum tunneling across an ultra-thin Atomic Layer Deposition (ALD) insulating barrier. Building upon our prior empirical validation of deep-cryogenic avalanche mechanics, this manuscript establishes the fundamental theoretical feasibility and structural foundation of the complete device. Signal transport simulations confirm that an undoped interface coupled with a strong drift field enables highly efficient, nanosecond-scale transient electron drift, resolving completely within 35 ns. The SuperEM architecture thus constitutes a scalable, high-resolution, and fast time-response framework for next-generation C$ν$B and LDM observatories.

</details>


### [114] [Silent coverage failures in rare-event searches and a degeneracy index that predicts them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09203)
*Davide Pagno*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了在低背景新物理搜寻中，模型偏差对置信区间覆盖概率的影响，引入Poisson-Fisher退化指数量化偏差方向与强度，并指出传统校准无法抵御模型误设，主张通过约束性辅助信息或包络建模来恢复覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低背景实验（如暗物质探测、无中微子双贝塔衰变）中，少量事件下的信号强度推断依赖于精确的统计模型；但实际数据生成过程与名义模拟器之间常存在未建模偏差，威胁置信区间的频率学覆盖性质。

Method: 采用精确泊松计数实验、暗物质反冲谱及0νββ峰搜索三类典型场景，系统评估六种区间构造法在受控模型偏离下的上下端点覆盖性能；提出Poisson–Fisher退化指数 I_PF(δν;ϑ₀)=(β,γ)，将期望计数形变投影至拟合切空间，以β表征信号偏移方向（单位：轮廓标准误差），γ表征未吸收残差的Poisson–Fisher范数；结合饱和泊松优度检验（5%第一类错误率）诊断可检测性。

Result: 正向信号类偏差损害发现侧覆盖但使上限保守；负向信号偏差（如信号效率高估）导致上限端点欠覆盖；名义模拟器校准不能防范其相对于真实数据生成机制的误设；大|β|小γ的形变易逃逸诊断，需以辅助信息约束对应 nuisance 或纳入稳健包络；在严格共线约束 nuisance 基准下，显式建模该形变可在网格点上恢复覆盖，但以区间灵敏度为代价。

Conclusion: 模型偏差的方向（β符号）决定哪一端点覆盖受损，而其可检测性由γ主导；稳健推断需超越标称模拟器校准，应将潜在形变参数化为受约束 nuisance 或嵌入可信包络，权衡覆盖保证与统计功效。

Abstract: Searches for new physics in low-background experiments infer a non-negative signal strength from few events and often report an upper limit. Nominal frequentist coverage requires both a valid interval construction and an adequate data model. We study how controlled model departures affect lower- and upper-endpoint coverage for six interval procedures in an exact Poisson counting experiment, dark-matter recoil spectra, and a neutrinoless-double-beta-decay peak search. We introduce the Poisson--Fisher degeneracy index $\mathcal I_{\mathrm{PF}}(δν;\vartheta_0)=(β,γ)$, which maps a specified expected-count deformation, after projection onto the complete fitted tangent space, to $β$, the signed fitted signal shift in profiled standard-error units, and $γ$, the Poisson--Fisher norm of the unabsorbed residual. Locally, the sign of $β$ identifies the threatened endpoint, while larger $γ$ implies greater detectability by the saturated-Poisson goodness-of-fit test used here at a fixed $5\%$ type-I error rate. Across the studied deformations, positive signal-like bias degrades discovery-side coverage while making upper limits conservative; negative signal bias from overestimated signal efficiency can make the upper endpoint undercover. Calibration under the nominal simulator does not protect against misspecification of that simulator relative to the data-generating process. A plausible deformation with large $|β|$ and small $γ$ may therefore evade diagnosis and should be represented by a nuisance constrained with auxiliary information or included in a defensible envelope. In the exactly collinear constrained-nuisance benchmark, modelling the deformation restores coverage at the evaluated grid points, at a quantifiable cost in interval sensitivity.

</details>


### [115] [Amplitude analysis for charmed meson decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09338)
*Han Zhang,Zhenxuan Li,Chunyi Guan,Zehui Lu,Hui Li,Liping Yang,Yechun Yu,Nan Zhang,Minggang Zhao,Yu Lu,Bai-Cian Ke,Liaoyuan Dong*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了BESIII合作组在多体含粲介子衰变振幅分析中的方法学，重点阐述无 bins 最大似然拟合的概率密度函数与似然函数构建、蒙特卡洛归一化、探测效率与分辨率修正、XGBoost 多维背景建模，以及强子/半轻子衰变的振幅形式（含标准共振传播子），并讨论拟合分数、运动学投影与统计误差估计等关键分析环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合多体含粲介子衰变实验测量与两体中间过程理论预言之间的差距，提升强子物理中非微扰QCD过程的模型约束能力。

Method: 基于无 bins 最大似然拟合的振幅分析框架；采用蒙特卡洛积分进行PDF归一化；联合建模探测效率、分辨率及多维背景（XGBoost）；使用相对论不变振幅形式，包含Breit–Wigner型共振传播子及自旋耦合结构。

Result: 建立了一套完整、可复现、适用于BESIII数据的振幅分析技术流程，涵盖从原始拟合到物理量提取（如分波贡献、拟合分数、运动学分布）的全链条实现细节。

Conclusion: 该方法学为高精度检验强子谱学、共振性质及QCD手征动力学提供了坚实工具基础，亦为未来类粲偶素及重味物理振幅分析树立了实践范式。

Abstract: Amplitude analysis bridges the gap between experimental measurements of multibody charmed-meson decays and theoretical predictions of intermediate two-body processes. This work presents a comprehensive overview of the amplitude-analysis methodology employed by the BESIII Collaboration, emphasizing practical implementation. We detail the construction of the probability density function and likelihood function for unbinned maximum-likelihood fits. This encompasses Monte Carlo integration techniques for normalization, the incorporation of detection efficiency and resolution effects, and multidimensional background modeling utilizing XGBoost classifiers. Furthermore, we describe the amplitude formalism for both hadronic and semileptonic decays, incorporating standard resonance-propagator parametrizations. Key analytical aspects, including the evaluation of fit fractions, the generation of kinematic projections, and the estimation of statistical uncertainties, are also discussed.

</details>


### [116] [Automated Signal Integrity Analysis Framework for High-Speed Interconnects in the PPCB-1347-MuPix11 Probe Card](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09462)
*D M S Sultan,R. Plackett,A. E. McDougall,A. J. A. Knight,A. S. Rotelli,J. Vossebeld,D. Bortoletto*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复用的MATLAB信号完整性（SI）框架，将四端口S参数转化为链路级可追溯证据，而非单一损耗指标；该框架在PPCB-1347-MuPix11探针卡的四条1.25 Gbps差分通道上验证，结合CST仿真S参数与虚拟时域求解器，完成混合模变换、路径长度感知损耗分解、因果信道建模及PRBS-31眼图/BER/8b10b分析；结果表明路径长度不足以表征SI性能，各通道在插入损耗、D2C抑制和眼宽上表现各异，BER均低于报告阈值，无法排序；框架强调波形行为、模型预测与实测数据的区分，支持从设计评审到校准VNA测量解释的可扩展SI对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SI分析常依赖单一损耗指标（如SDD21），难以全面反映高速互连链路的真实性能；需建立可追溯、可复用、支持多维评估（眼图、BER、编码效应等）的链路级分析框架，以支撑设计评审与实测数据的物理意义解读。

Method: 基于MATLAB构建自动化SI分析流水线：预检文件结构 → 功率归一化混合模变换（S→ABCD→混合模S参数）→ 路径长度感知的差分/共模损耗分解 → 构建因果性约束的信道模型（含相位/群延迟校正）→ 时域卷积驱动PRBS-31激励 → 输出眼图、条件BER、8b10b编码链路性能。输入为VNA实测或CST Microwave 3D-Solver仿真的四端口S参数。

Result: 在1.25 Gbps（奈奎斯特0.625 GHz）下，DP1–DP4通道的SDD21为−0.350至−0.300 dB，D2C转换为−31.038至−28.236 dBc，建模FEB输入眼宽为0.586–0.588 V；DP3具最低奈奎斯特插入损耗，DP1具最优D2C隔离，DP4具最大眼宽；所有分析BER均低于报告下限（<1e−12），无法排序。

Conclusion: 路径长度不能作为SI性能的充分排序依据；该框架通过保留波形行为、模型预测与有限记录观测的本体区分，实现了从设计到校准测量的可追溯、多维度、可扩展SI分析范式，超越了传统单指标评估。

Abstract: A reusable MATLAB signal-integrity (SI) framework is presented that converts compatible four-port S-parameter data, measured by VNA or obtained from electromagnetic simulation, into traceable link-level evidence rather than a single loss metric. The framework is demonstrated on the four 1.25 Gbps differential routes (DP1-DP4) of the PPCB-1347-MuPix11 probe card using PTSL CST Microwave 3D-Solver-derived four-port S-parameters and a virtual time-domain solver. The automated pipeline preflights file structures, performs a power-normalized mixed-mode transformation, applies route-length-aware loss decomposition, constructs a causally loaded channel model, and propagates full PRBS-31 sequences into eye-diagram, conditional-BER, and 8b10b-coded-link analyses. At the 1.25 Gbps data rate (Nyquist 0.625 GHz), DP1-DP4 exhibit differential insertion loss (SDD21) from -0.350 to -0.300 dB, differential-to-common conversion from -31.038 to -28.236 dBc, and modeled FEB-input eye openings from 0.586 to 0.588 V. The comparison shows that path length alone is not an adequate SI ranking variable: DP3 has the lowest Nyquist insertion loss, DP1 the strongest differential-to-common isolation, and DP4 the largest modeled eye. All analytical BER values remain below the reporting floor and therefore do not support a BER ranking. By preserving the distinction between route-dependent waveform behavior, model projections, and finite-record observations, the framework provides an extensible basis for comparative high-speed-interconnect SI analysis from design review through calibrated VNA measurement interpretation.

</details>

### [117] [Chemical Potential and Analytic Continuation for Non-Hermitian Lattice Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08862)
*Chen-Te Ma,Hui Zhang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出非厄米晶格费米子的化学势定义，证明在偶数味、简并质量及配对化学势条件下可避免符号问题；通过一维自由费米子模型揭示符号问题是数值障碍而非物理本质，并导出有限格点间距下解析、连续极限下非解析的精确传播子；最后利用AI辅助拟合实现虚化学势到实化学势的解析延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非厄米系统中蒙特卡洛模拟面临的严重符号问题，探索其物理根源与数值处理新方法。

Method: 构造配对化学势（μ,−μ）或（iμ,iμ）的非厄米晶格费米子体系；严格求解一维自由费米子传播子；结合Hybrid Monte Carlo算法与AI驱动的解析延拓技术。

Result: 证明偶数风味下特定化学势配对可完全消除符号问题；揭示符号问题源于离散化导致的数值病态，而非连续极限下的内在物理障碍；获得有限格距下解析、连续极限下出现分支点的传播子表达式；实现高精度虚→实化学势解析延拓。

Conclusion: 非厄米晶格费米子的符号问题具有可控性与可规避性；其本质是离散化引起的数值障碍，可通过适当对称性设计与AI增强分析予以克服；为强关联非厄米量子多体模拟提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce a chemical potential for non-Hermitian lattice fermions and show that, for even flavors with degenerate masses and paired chemical potentials $(μ,-μ)$ or $(iμ,iμ)$, the Hybrid Monte Carlo algorithm is free of the sign problem. For one-dimensional free fermions, we demonstrate that the sign problem is a numerical rather than physical obstruction and derive the exact propagator, which is analytic at finite lattice spacing away from its poles but becomes non-analytic in the continuum limit. Finally, we use AI-assisted fitting to perform analytic continuation from imaginary to real chemical potentials.

</details>
